A body-less step of only outcomes is a pure decision.\\nworkflow sort_ticket\\n  input urgent: Bool\\n  outcome fast: type String, route success\\n  outcome slow: type String, route success\\n\\nstep triage\\n  outcome now: when urgent, route fast(\\\"page\\\")\\n  outcome later: otherwise, route slow(\\\"queue\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"declaration\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"top-level\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"after\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Names the steps a step waits for, so the dependency is written rather than inferred from position.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `verify` waits for `build`: the dependency is written, not inferred.\\nworkflow gated_build\\n  input tag: String\\n  outcome done: type Checked, route success\\n\\ntype Built   { artifact: String }\\ntype Checked { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker ci\\n  action build(tag: String) -> Built\\n  action verify(artifact: String) -> Checked\\n\\nstep build\\n  build(tag: tag) -> built\\n\\nstep verify after build\\n  verify(artifact: built.artifact) -> checked\\n  route done(checked)\",\n        \"//! A diamond: build and notes run independently, publish waits for both.\\nworkflow release_notes\\n  input tag: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Build { artifact: String }\\ntype Notes { text: String }\\n\\nworker release\\n  action build(tag: String) -> Build\\n  action render_notes(tag: String) -> Notes\\n\\nstep start\\n  \\\"release \\\" + tag -> label\\n\\nstep build after start\\n  build(tag: tag) -> built\\n\\nstep notes after start\\n  render_notes(tag: tag) -> rendered\\n\\nstep publish after build, notes\\n  route done(label + \\\" \\\" + built.artifact + rendered.text)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"fork\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Runs work concurrently inside one step: `fork item in items` one branch per item, or a bare `fork` for named branches.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! One branch per repo, checked in parallel, joined in input order.\\nworkflow sweep_repos\\n  input repos: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [Verdict], route success\\n\\ntype Verdict { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker ci\\n  action check(repo: String) -> Verdict\\n\\nstep sweep\\n  fork repo in repos\\n    check(repo: repo)\\n  join -> verdicts\\n  route done(verdicts)\",\n        \"//! A bare fork runs named heterogeneous calls concurrently.\\nworkflow load_dashboard\\n  input user_id: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Profile { name: String }\\ntype History { entries: Int }\\n\\nworker accounts\\n  action fetch_profile(id: String) -> Profile\\n  action fetch_history(id: String) -> History\\n\\nstep load\\n  fork\\n    fetch_profile(id: user_id) -> profile\\n    fetch_history(id: user_id) -> history\\n  join\\n  outcome busy: when history.entries > 3, route done(profile.name + \\\" is busy\\\")\\n  outcome quiet: otherwise, route done(profile.name)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 8,\n        \"title\": \"Parallel and sequential work\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §8 — Parallel and sequential work\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"join\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Closes a `fork`, binding a collection fork's results in input order.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `join` closes the fork, binding the branches' results in input order.\\nworkflow gather_reports\\n  input repos: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [Report], route success\\n\\ntype Report { summary: String }\\n\\nworker inspectors\\n  action inspect(repo: String) -> Report\\n\\nstep gather\\n  fork repo in repos\\n    inspect(repo: repo)\\n  join -> reports\\n  route done(reports)\",\n        \"//! Bare `join` closes named branches; there is no collection to bind.\\nworkflow warm_caches\\n  input region: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nworker infra\\n  action warm_dns(region: String) -> Nil\\n  action warm_cdn(region: String) -> Nil\\n\\nstep warm\\n  fork\\n    warm_dns(region: region)\\n    warm_cdn(region: region)\\n  join\\n  route done(region)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 8,\n        \"title\": \"Parallel and sequential work\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §8 — Parallel and sequential work\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"loop\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Runs a bounded value-threading loop whose body runs at least once and must rebind the threaded value.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Improve the draft until it is ready, at most three passes.\\nworkflow refine_draft\\n  input topic: String\\n  outcome done:  type Draft,  route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Draft { body: String, ready: Bool }\\n\\nworker writers\\n  action improve(topic: String, prior: Draft) -> Draft\\n\\nstep refine\\n  loop draft = Draft(body: \\\"\\\", ready: false)\\n    improve(topic: topic, prior: draft) -> draft\\n    until draft.ready\\n    max 3\\n  outcome ready: when draft.ready, route done(draft)\\n  outcome exhausted: otherwise, route stuck(\\\"the draft never settled\\\")\",\n        \"//! The threaded value carries state between passes; rounds are counted.\\nworkflow tune_model\\n  input target: String\\n  outcome done:  type Int,    route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Tuning { score: Int, good_enough: Bool }\\n\\nworker lab\\n  action tune(target: String, prior: Tuning) -> Tuning\\n\\nstep tune\\n  loop tuning = Tuning(score: 0, good_enough: false) counting rounds\\n    tune(target: target, prior: tuning) -> tuning\\n    until tuning.good_enough\\n    max 8\\n  outcome tuned: when tuning.good_enough, route done(rounds)\\n  outcome gave_up: otherwise, route stuck(target)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 9,\n        \"title\": \"Bounded iteration\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §9 — Bounded iteration\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"counting\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Binds the number of completed iterations of a `loop`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `counting` binds how many iterations the loop completed.\\nworkflow poll_job\\n  input job: String\\n  outcome done:  type Int,    route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Status { ready: Bool }\\n\\nworker jobs\\n  action poll(job: String, prior: Status) -> Status\\n\\nstep watch\\n  loop status = Status(ready: false) counting polls\\n    poll(job: job, prior: status) -> status\\n    until status.ready\\n    max 10\\n  outcome ready: when status.ready, route done(polls)\\n  outcome gave_up: otherwise, route stuck(job)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 9,\n        \"title\": \"Bounded iteration\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §9 — Bounded iteration\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"until\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"States the condition that ends a `loop`, tested after the body has run.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `until` ends the loop, tested after each pass of the body.\\nworkflow stabilize_build\\n  input tag: String\\n  outcome done:  type Build,  route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Build { green: Bool }\\n\\nworker ci\\n  action rebuild(tag: String, prior: Build) -> Build\\n\\nstep converge\\n  loop build = Build(green: false)\\n    rebuild(tag: tag, prior: build) -> build\\n    until build.green\\n    max 5\\n  outcome green: when build.green, route done(build)\\n  outcome exhausted: otherwise, route stuck(tag)\",\n        \"//! A compound condition: stop only when ready AND approved.\\nworkflow draft_and_signoff\\n  input topic: String\\n  outcome done:  type Draft,  route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Draft { ready: Bool, approved: Bool }\\n\\nworker desks\\n  action revise(topic: String, prior: Draft) -> Draft\\n\\nstep converge\\n  loop draft = Draft(ready: false, approved: false)\\n    revise(topic: topic, prior: draft) -> draft\\n    until draft.ready and draft.approved\\n    max 6\\n  outcome settled: when draft.ready and draft.approved, route done(draft)\\n  outcome exhausted: otherwise, route stuck(topic)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 9,\n        \"title\": \"Bounded iteration\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §9 — Bounded iteration\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"max\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Bounds iteration: the mandatory ceiling on a `loop`, or `max N visits` on a step inside a flow loop.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `max` is the mandatory ceiling; exhaustion is a distinct outcome.\\nworkflow negotiate_quote\\n  input request: String\\n  outcome done:  type Offer,  route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Offer { accepted: Bool }\\n\\nworker brokers\\n  action haggle(request: String, prior: Offer) -> Offer\\n\\nstep negotiate\\n  loop offer = Offer(accepted: false)\\n    haggle(request: request, prior: offer) -> offer\\n    until offer.accepted\\n    max 4\\n  outcome agreed: when offer.accepted, route done(offer)\\n  outcome exhausted: otherwise, route stuck(request)\",\n        \"//! The other place `max` stands: a visit bound on a step in a flow loop.\\nworkflow polish_copy\\n  input text: String\\n  outcome done: type Verdict, route success\\n\\ntype Verdict { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker desks\\n  action review(text: String) -> Verdict\\n\\nstep review\\n  review(text: text) -> verdict\\n\\nstep judge\\n  outcome again: when not verdict.ok and visits < 2, route review\\n  outcome settled: otherwise, route done(verdict)\\n  max 2 visits\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 9,\n        \"title\": \"Bounded iteration\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §9 — Bounded iteration\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"sequential\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Runs a collection `fork` one item at a time in input order instead of in parallel.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `sequential` publishes one target at a time, in input order.\\nworkflow publish_targets\\n  input targets: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [Receipt], route success\\n\\ntype Receipt { target: String }\\n\\nworker publisher\\n  action publish(target: String) -> Receipt\\n\\nstep publish_all\\n  fork target in targets sequential\\n    publish(target: target)\\n  join -> receipts\\n  route done(receipts)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 8,\n        \"title\": \"Parallel and sequential work\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §8 — Parallel and sequential work\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"spawn\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Starts a child workflow without waiting for it, so it can bind no result.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Start a child without waiting; it can bind no result.\\nworkflow archive_report\\n  input report_id: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nchild notify_archived(report_id: String) -> Nil\\n\\nstep archive\\n  spawn notify_archived(report_id: report_id)\\n  route done(report_id)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 5,\n        \"title\": \"Children and subflows\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §5 — Children and subflows\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"wait\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Waits for a declared signal and binds it; a `timeout` makes that binding optional, because expiry is absence.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Take delivery of the declared signal, binding its payload.\\nworkflow hold_for_go\\n  input build_id: String\\n  signal go: Approval\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Approval { approver: String }\\n\\nstep hold\\n  wait go -> approval\\n  route done(approval.approver + \\\" released \\\" + build_id)\",\n        \"//! A timed wait binds an optional, because expiry is absence.\\nworkflow ask_signoff\\n  input change_id: String\\n  signal signoff: Signoff\\n  outcome shipped:   type String, route success\\n  outcome escalated: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Signoff { reviewer: String }\\n\\nstep hold\\n  wait signoff timeout 2d -> decision\\n  outcome approved: when decision is present, route shipped(decision.reviewer)\\n  outcome lapsed: otherwise, route escalated(\\\"lapsed: \\\" + change_id)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 10,\n        \"title\": \"Waits, timeouts, signals, and sleep\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §10 — Waits, timeouts, signals, and sleep\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"sleep\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Sleeps for a duration on a durable timer.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Sleep on a durable timer; the run survives a restart mid-sleep.\\nworkflow cool_down\\n  input build_id: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep pause\\n  sleep 30s\\n  route done(build_id)\",\n        \"//! Sleep between polls: a durable timer inside the loop body.\\nworkflow await_export\\n  input job: String\\n  outcome done:  type Status, route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Status { finished: Bool }\\n\\nworker exports\\n  action poll(job: String, prior: Status) -> Status\\n\\nstep watch\\n  loop status = Status(finished: false)\\n    sleep 30s\\n    poll(job: job, prior: status) -> status\\n    until status.finished\\n    max 20\\n  outcome finished: when status.finished, route done(status)\\n  outcome timed_out: otherwise, route stuck(job)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 10,\n        \"title\": \"Waits, timeouts, signals, and sleep\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §10 — Waits, timeouts, signals, and sleep\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"timeout\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Bounds the workflow run in the header, one activity on an action or call, or one `wait` — three distinct places.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! The header timeout bounds the whole run.\\nworkflow nightly_sweep\\n  timeout 2h\\n  input target: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep sweep\\n  route done(\\\"swept \\\" + target)\",\n        \"//! An activity timeout on the declaration, tightened at one call site.\\nworkflow build_twice\\n  input tag: String\\n  outcome done: type Build, route success\\n\\ntype Build { artifact: String }\\n\\nworker builders\\n  action build(tag: String) -> Build\\n    timeout 30m\\n\\nstep quick_probe\\n  build(tag: tag) -> probe\\n    timeout 5m\\n\\nstep full_build\\n  build(tag: probe.artifact) -> built\\n  route done(built)\",\n        \"//! A wait with a timeout binds an optional: expiry is absence.\\nworkflow hold_for_signoff\\n  input change_id: String\\n  signal signoff: Signoff\\n  outcome shipped:   type String, route success\\n  outcome escalated: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Signoff { reviewer: String }\\n\\nstep hold\\n  wait signoff timeout 2d -> decision\\n  outcome approved: when decision is present, route shipped(decision.reviewer)\\n  outcome lapsed: otherwise, route escalated(\\\"window lapsed: \\\" + change_id)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 4,\n        \"title\": \"Workers, actions, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §4 — Workers, actions, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"workflow-header\",\n        \"worker-block\",\n        \"step-body\",\n        \"signal-reference\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"retry\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Sets an action's retry policy, as `retry N every DURATION` or `retry N backoff MIN..MAX`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Retry the flaky pull three times before the step faults.\\nworkflow sync_mirror\\n  input remote: String\\n  outcome done: type Synced, route success\\n\\ntype Synced { revision: String }\\n\\nworker mirrors\\n  action pull(remote: String) -> Synced\\n    retry 3 every 30s\\n\\nstep pull\\n  pull(remote: remote) -> synced\\n  route done(synced)\",\n        \"//! The growing form: intervals stretch from five seconds toward four minutes.\\nworkflow crawl_source\\n  input source: String\\n  outcome done: type Page, route success\\n\\ntype Page { body: String }\\n\\nworker crawlers\\n  action crawl(source: String) -> Page\\n    retry 6 backoff 5s..4m\\n\\nstep crawl\\n  crawl(source: source) -> page\\n  route done(page)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 4,\n        \"title\": \"Workers, actions, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §4 — Workers, actions, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"worker-block\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"advisory\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Marks an action whose failure never faults its calling step; it can bind no result and be no pipe stage.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! An advisory announcement can fail without faulting the run.\\nworkflow ship_build\\n  input version: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nworker notifier\\n  action announce(version: String) -> Nil\\n    advisory, timeout 30s\\n\\nstep ship\\n  announce(version: version)\\n  route done(version)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 4,\n        \"title\": \"Workers, actions, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §4 — Workers, actions, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"worker-block\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"agent\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Marks an action as an agent seam: its one `String` parameter carries a prompt and its declared result carries accumulated `text`, last-answer `final_message`, `stop_reason`, and `session_id`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! An agent action hands its prompt to a harness and binds the outcome\\n//! record: accumulated `text`, the last answer as `final_message`, the canonical\\n//! `stop_reason`, and the `session_id` handle of the conversation that produced it.\\nworkflow ask_reviewer\\n  input question: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Reply { text: String, final_message: String, stop_reason: String, session_id: String }\\n\\nworker assistants\\n  action ask(prompt: String) -> Reply\\n    agent, timeout 10m\\n\\nstep ask\\n  ask(prompt: question) -> reply\\n  route done(reply.text)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 4,\n        \"title\": \"Workers, actions, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §4 — Workers, actions, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"worker-block\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"every\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Sets the fixed interval between retries in `retry N every DURATION`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `every` fixes the interval between retries: ten seconds, each time.\\nworkflow ping_service\\n  input url: String\\n  outcome done: type Pong, route success\\n\\ntype Pong { latency_ms: Int }\\n\\nworker probes\\n  action ping(url: String) -> Pong\\n    retry 5 every 10s\\n\\nstep ping\\n  ping(url: url) -> pong\\n  route done(pong)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 4,\n        \"title\": \"Workers, actions, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §4 — Workers, actions, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"worker-block\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"backoff\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Sets the growing retry interval in `retry N backoff MIN..MAX`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `backoff` grows the retry interval from ten seconds toward two minutes.\\nworkflow fetch_upstream\\n  input source: String\\n  outcome done: type Fetched, route success\\n\\ntype Fetched { bytes: Int }\\n\\nworker fetchers\\n  action fetch(source: String) -> Fetched\\n    retry 4 backoff 10s..2m\\n\\nstep fetch\\n  fetch(source: source) -> fetched\\n  route done(fetched)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 4,\n        \"title\": \"Workers, actions, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §4 — Workers, actions, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"worker-block\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"node\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Pins dispatch to a named worker node, on the action declaration or overridden at a call site.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Pin an action's dispatch to the named worker node.\\nworkflow build_release\\n  input tag: String\\n  outcome done: type Build, route success\\n\\ntype Build { artifact: String }\\n\\nworker builders\\n  action build(tag: String) -> Build\\n    node linux_large\\n\\nstep build\\n  build(tag: tag) -> built\\n  route done(built)\",\n        \"//! Override the declared node at one urgent call site.\\nworkflow hotfix_build\\n  input tag: String\\n  outcome done: type Build, route success\\n\\ntype Build { artifact: String }\\n\\nworker builders\\n  action build(tag: String) -> Build\\n    node standard, timeout 30m\\n\\nstep build\\n  build(tag: tag) -> built\\n    node urgent, timeout 5m\\n  route done(built)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 4,\n        \"title\": \"Workers, actions, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §4 — Workers, actions, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"worker-block\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"on\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Opens `on failure`, the block a step runs when it faults, which must itself end in a route.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `on failure` runs when the step faults, and must end in a route.\\nworkflow publish_package\\n  input name: String\\n  outcome done:   type String, route success\\n  outcome failed: type String, route failure\\n\\nworker registry\\n  action publish(name: String) -> Nil\\n  action yank(name: String) -> Nil\\n\\nstep publish\\n  publish(name: name)\\n  route done(name)\\n\\n  on failure\\n    yank(name: name)\\n    route failed(\\\"publish failed; yanked\\\")\",\n        \"//! Compensation may route back into the graph, not only to an outcome.\\nworkflow deploy_with_rollback\\n  input build: String\\n  outcome done:   type String, route success\\n  outcome failed: type String, route failure\\n\\nworker ops\\n  action deploy(build: String) -> Nil\\n  action rollback(build: String) -> Nil\\n  action report(build: String) -> Nil\\n\\nstep deploy\\n  deploy(build: build)\\n  route done(build)\\n\\n  on failure\\n    rollback(build: build)\\n    route notify\\n\\nstep notify\\n  report(build: build)\\n  route failed(\\\"deploy failed; rolled back: \\\" + build)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 11,\n        \"title\": \"Failure handling\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §11 — Failure handling\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"failure\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Maps a declared outcome to the engine's failed status, and after `on` names a step's compensation block.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `route failure` marks the outcome that fails the run.\\nworkflow validate_order\\n  input order_id: String\\n  input valid: Bool\\n  outcome accepted: type String, route success\\n  outcome rejected: type String, route failure\\n\\nstep validate\\n  outcome ok: when valid, route accepted(order_id)\\n  outcome bad: otherwise, route rejected(\\\"invalid order: \\\" + order_id)\",\n        \"//! After `on`, the same word names a step's compensation block.\\nworkflow guard_import\\n  input dataset: String\\n  outcome done:   type String, route success\\n  outcome failed: type String, route failure\\n\\nworker importers\\n  action import_rows(dataset: String) -> Nil\\n\\nstep import\\n  import_rows(dataset: dataset)\\n  route done(dataset)\\n\\n  on failure\\n    route failed(\\\"import faulted: \\\" + dataset)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 3,\n        \"title\": \"Workflow header\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §3 — Workflow header\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"workflow-header\",\n        \"step-body\",\n        \"route-target\",\n        \"step-reference\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"when\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Guards an outcome arm; the first true guard wins, and its proofs narrow optionals through that arm.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! The first true `when` wins; its proofs hold through the arm.\\nworkflow admit_finding\\n  input finding: Finding\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Finding { blocking: Bool, detail: String? }\\n\\nstep judge\\n  outcome real: when finding.detail is present, route done(finding.detail)\\n  outcome noise: otherwise, route done(\\\"no detail recorded\\\")\",\n        \"//! Guards over every enum variant are total; no `otherwise` needed.\\nworkflow route_ticket\\n  input ticket: Ticket\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Category = Urgent | Routine | Spam\\ntype Ticket { category: Category }\\n\\nstep route_it\\n  outcome page: when ticket.category == Urgent, route done(\\\"page\\\")\\n  outcome queue: when ticket.category == Routine, route done(\\\"queue\\\")\\n  outcome bin: when ticket.category == Spam, route done(\\\"discard\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 7,\n        \"title\": \"Selection, outcomes, and routes\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §7 — Selection, outcomes, and routes\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"otherwise\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"The complement arm of a guarded outcome set, which must come last.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `otherwise` is the complement arm, and must come last.\\nworkflow rate_change\\n  input risky: Bool\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep rate\\n  outcome careful: when risky, route done(\\\"hold for review\\\")\\n  outcome fine: otherwise, route done(\\\"ship it\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 7,\n        \"title\": \"Selection, outcomes, and routes\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §7 — Selection, outcomes, and routes\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"route\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Sends flow to another step or to a declared outcome, supplying that outcome's payload.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `route` sends flow to another step or to a declared outcome.\\nworkflow escalate_alert\\n  input urgent: Bool\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep assess\\n  outcome page: when urgent, route page_operator\\n  outcome log: otherwise, route done(\\\"logged\\\")\\n\\nstep page_operator\\n  route done(\\\"paged\\\")\",\n        \"//! A backward route makes a flow loop; the cycle carries a visit bound.\\nworkflow retry_gate\\n  input repo: String\\n  outcome done:   type Verdict, route success\\n  outcome failed: type String,  route failure\\n\\ntype Verdict { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker ci\\n  action check(repo: String) -> Verdict\\n\\nstep attempt\\n  check(repo: repo) -> verdict\\n\\nstep judge\\n  outcome again: when not verdict.ok and visits < 3, route attempt\\n  outcome good: when verdict.ok, route done(verdict)\\n  outcome exhausted: otherwise, route failed(repo)\\n  max 3 visits\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 7,\n        \"title\": \"Selection, outcomes, and routes\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §7 — Selection, outcomes, and routes\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"workflow-header\",\n        \"step-body\",\n        \"pipe-stage\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"success\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Maps a declared outcome to the engine's completed status.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `route success` maps the outcome to the engine's completed status.\\nworkflow finish_job\\n  input job: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep wrap\\n  route done(\\\"finished \\\" + job)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 3,\n        \"title\": \"Workflow header\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §3 — Workflow header\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"workflow-header\",\n        \"route-target\",\n        \"step-reference\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"filter\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Keeps the elements of a collection its predicate accepts.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Keep only the findings the predicate accepts.\\nworkflow keep_blockers\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done: type [Finding], route success\\n\\ntype Finding { title: String, blocking: Bool }\\n\\nstep sift\\n  findings |> filter(.blocking) -> blockers\\n  route done(blockers)\",\n        \"//! A filter stage inside a longer pipe, feeding a tally.\\nworkflow measure_noise\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done:   type String, route success\\n  outcome failed: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Finding { resolved: Bool }\\n\\nstep measure\\n  findings |> filter(.resolved) |> count -> settled\\n  findings |> count -> total\\n  outcome all_settled: when settled == total, route done(\\\"all settled\\\")\\n  outcome open_items: otherwise, route failed(\\\"unresolved findings remain\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"pipeline-stage\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"pipe-stage\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"map\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Rewrites each element of a collection through a deterministic value expression.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Rewrite each element through a deterministic expression.\\nworkflow list_titles\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done: type [String], route success\\n\\ntype Finding { title: String, blocking: Bool }\\n\\nstep rename\\n  findings |> map(.title) -> titles\\n  route done(titles)\",\n        \"//! Combinators chain: filter first, then project the titles.\\nworkflow blocker_titles\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done: type [String], route success\\n\\ntype Finding { title: String, blocking: Bool }\\n\\nstep summarize\\n  findings |> filter(.blocking) |> map(.title) -> titles\\n  route done(titles)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"pipeline-stage\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"pipe-stage\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"sort\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Orders a collection by a key, which the compile refuses unless it is `Int`, `Float`, or `String`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Order a collection by a comparable key.\\nworkflow rank_findings\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done: type [Finding], route success\\n\\ntype Finding { title: String, severity: Int }\\n\\nstep rank\\n  findings |> sort(.severity) -> ranked\\n  route done(ranked)\",\n        \"//! Sort by a key, then take the front of the ordered list.\\nworkflow pick_leader\\n  input candidates: [Candidate]\\n  outcome done: type Candidate, route success\\n\\ntype Candidate { name: String, score: Int }\\n\\nstep choose\\n  candidates |> sort(.score) -> ranked\\n  ranked[0] -> leader\\n  route done(leader)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"pipeline-stage\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"pipe-stage\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"count\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Counts a collection's elements.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Count a collection's elements.\\nworkflow tally_findings\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done: type Int, route success\\n\\ntype Finding { title: String }\\n\\nstep tally\\n  findings |> count -> total\\n  route done(total)\",\n        \"//! Two tallies, compared in a guard.\\nworkflow judge_sweep\\n  input receipts: [Receipt]\\n  outcome done:   type String, route success\\n  outcome failed: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Receipt { ok: Bool }\\n\\nstep judge\\n  receipts |> count -> attempted\\n  receipts |> filter(.ok) |> count -> succeeded\\n  outcome full: when succeeded == attempted, route done(\\\"all landed\\\")\\n  outcome partial: otherwise, route failed(\\\"some failed\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"pipeline-stage\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"pipe-stage\",\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"any\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"True when at least one element of a collection satisfies its predicate.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! True when at least one element satisfies the predicate.\\nworkflow spot_blocker\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Finding { blocking: Bool }\\n\\nstep look\\n  findings |> any(.blocking) -> has_blocker\\n  outcome blocked: when has_blocker, route done(\\\"blocked\\\")\\n  outcome clear: otherwise, route done(\\\"clear\\\")\",\n        \"//! The predicate can be compound.\\nworkflow flag_hard_blockers\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Finding { blocking: Bool, severity: String }\\n\\nstep look\\n  findings |> any(.blocking and .severity == \\\"blocker\\\") -> has_hard_blocker\\n  outcome blocked: when has_hard_blocker, route done(\\\"hard blocker present\\\")\\n  outcome clear: otherwise, route done(\\\"no hard blockers\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"pipeline-stage\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"pipe-stage\",\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"all\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"True when every element of a collection satisfies its predicate.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! True when every element satisfies the predicate.\\nworkflow verify_receipts\\n  input receipts: [Receipt]\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Receipt { ok: Bool }\\n\\nstep verify\\n  receipts |> all(.ok) -> all_ok\\n  outcome good: when all_ok, route done(\\\"every receipt landed\\\")\\n  outcome partial: otherwise, route done(\\\"at least one failed\\\")\",\n        \"//! Universal claims read well with `or`: passed, or explicitly waived.\\nworkflow verify_waivers\\n  input checks: [Check]\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Check { passed: Bool, waived: Bool }\\n\\nstep verify\\n  checks |> all(.passed or .waived) -> acceptable\\n  outcome good: when acceptable, route done(\\\"release may proceed\\\")\\n  outcome bad: otherwise, route done(\\\"unwaived failure present\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"pipeline-stage\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"pipe-stage\",\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"is\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Opens a predicate: `is empty`, `is present`, or `is absent`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `is` opens a predicate: `is empty`, `is present`, or `is absent`.\\nworkflow check_queue\\n  input items: [String]\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep look\\n  outcome idle: when items is empty, route done(\\\"idle\\\")\\n  outcome busy: otherwise, route done(\\\"busy\\\")\",\n        \"//! `is present` proves an optional before it is read.\\nworkflow read_manifest\\n  input manifest: Manifest\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Manifest { name: String, notes: String? }\\n\\nstep read\\n  outcome noted: when manifest.notes is present, route done(manifest.notes)\\n  outcome bare: otherwise, route done(manifest.name)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"empty\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"The predicate for a collection with nothing in it.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! The predicate for a collection with nothing in it.\\nworkflow judge_review\\n  input findings: [String]\\n  outcome clean:   type String, route success\\n  outcome blocked: type String, route failure\\n\\nstep judge\\n  outcome pass: when findings is empty, route clean(\\\"no findings\\\")\\n  outcome fail: otherwise, route blocked(\\\"findings remain\\\")\",\n        \"//! Emptiness after filtering: no blockers left means clear.\\nworkflow clear_to_land\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done:   type String, route success\\n  outcome failed: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Finding { blocking: Bool }\\n\\nstep decide\\n  findings |> filter(.blocking) -> blockers\\n  outcome clear: when blockers is empty, route done(\\\"clear to land\\\")\\n  outcome blocked: otherwise, route failed(\\\"blockers remain\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"present\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Proves an optional access path present, narrowing it from `T?` to `T` wherever that proof holds.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Prove the optional present; the proof narrows `T?` to `T` in the arm.\\nworkflow read_note\\n  input finding: Finding\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Finding { title: String, note: String? }\\n\\nstep read\\n  outcome noted: when finding.note is present, route done(finding.note)\\n  outcome bare: otherwise, route done(finding.title)\",\n        \"//! Proofs chain: the first makes the record real, the second its field.\\nworkflow read_deep_note\\n  input report: Report\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Detail { note: String? }\\ntype Report { title: String, detail: Detail? }\\n\\nstep read\\n  outcome noted: when report.detail is present and report.detail.note is present,\\n    route done(report.detail.note)\\n  outcome bare: otherwise, route done(report.title)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"absent\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Proves an optional access path absent, which is what an `or` chain narrows on its false edge.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Prove the optional absent, which is what omission looks like.\\nworkflow chase_signoff\\n  input review: Review\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Review { change: String, verdict: String? }\\n\\nstep chase\\n  outcome pending: when review.verdict is absent, route done(\\\"chase \\\" + review.change)\\n  outcome settled: otherwise, route done(review.change)\",\n        \"//! Expiry IS absence: a lapsed timed wait proves the binding absent.\\nworkflow chase_reply\\n  input ticket: String\\n  signal reply: Reply\\n  outcome done:      type String, route success\\n  outcome escalated: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Reply { text: String }\\n\\nstep hold\\n  wait reply timeout 1d -> response\\n  outcome silent: when response is absent, route escalated(\\\"no reply: \\\" + ticket)\\n  outcome answered: otherwise, route done(\\\"answered: \\\" + ticket)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"not\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Negates a boolean expression.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Negate a boolean expression.\\nworkflow gate_ship\\n  input checks_passed: Bool\\n  outcome held:    type String, route failure\\n  outcome shipped: type String, route success\\n\\nstep decide\\n  outcome hold: when not checks_passed, route held(\\\"checks failed\\\")\\n  outcome go: otherwise, route shipped(\\\"shipped\\\")\",\n        \"//! Negation inside a quantifier's predicate.\\nworkflow find_open_items\\n  input findings: [Finding]\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Finding { resolved: Bool }\\n\\nstep look\\n  findings |> any(not .resolved) -> has_open\\n  outcome open: when has_open, route done(\\\"open items remain\\\")\\n  outcome settled: otherwise, route done(\\\"all resolved\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"and\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Boolean conjunction, where each conjunct's presence proof holds for the operands after it.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Both conjuncts must hold; each proof carries to the operands after it.\\nworkflow admit_report\\n  input report: Report\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Report { blocking: Bool, detail: String? }\\n\\nstep admit\\n  outcome real: when report.blocking and report.detail is present, route done(report.detail)\\n  outcome noise: otherwise, route done(\\\"nothing blocking\\\")\",\n        \"//! Plain conjunction: both flags must hold.\\nworkflow admit_release\\n  input build: Build\\n  outcome done:   type String, route success\\n  outcome failed: type String, route failure\\n\\ntype Build { tests_green: Bool, signed: Bool }\\n\\nstep admit\\n  outcome ship: when build.tests_green and build.signed, route done(\\\"shipped\\\")\\n  outcome hold: otherwise, route failed(\\\"not ready\\\")\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"or\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Boolean disjunction, where each disjunct's absence proof holds on its false edge.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Either disjunct admits; absence proofs hold on the false edge.\\nworkflow accept_change\\n  input change: Change\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Change { approved: Bool, urgent: Bool, title: String }\\n\\nstep judge\\n  outcome go: when change.approved or change.urgent, route done(change.title)\\n  outcome no: otherwise, route done(\\\"declined: \\\" + change.title)\",\n        \"//! On the false edge of `is absent`, the right side reads the value safely.\\nworkflow honor_waiver\\n  input request: Request\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Waiver  { approved: Bool }\\ntype Request { title: String, waiver: Waiver? }\\n\\nstep judge\\n  outcome waved: when request.waiver is absent or request.waiver.approved,\\n    route done(\\\"proceed: \\\" + request.title)\\n  outcome held: otherwise, route done(\\\"held: \\\" + request.title)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 6,\n        \"title\": \"Steps, dependencies, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §6 — Steps, dependencies, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"true\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"The `Bool` literal for truth.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `true` is the Bool literal for truth.\\nworkflow flag_finding\\n  input title: String\\n  outcome done: type Finding, route success\\n\\ntype Finding { title: String, blocking: Bool }\\n\\nstep flag\\n  route done(title: title, blocking: true)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 2,\n        \"title\": \"Values and types\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §2 — Values and types\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"false\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"The `Bool` literal for falsity.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `false` is the Bool literal for falsity.\\nworkflow clear_finding\\n  input title: String\\n  outcome done: type Finding, route success\\n\\ntype Finding { title: String, blocking: Bool }\\n\\nstep clear\\n  route done(title: title, blocking: false)\",\n        \"//! `false` seeds the loop's threaded value before the first pass.\\nworkflow chase_approval\\n  input request: String\\n  outcome done:  type Verdict, route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String,  route failure\\n\\ntype Verdict { approved: Bool }\\n\\nworker desks\\n  action press(request: String, prior: Verdict) -> Verdict\\n\\nstep chase\\n  loop verdict = Verdict(approved: false)\\n    press(request: request, prior: verdict) -> verdict\\n    until verdict.approved\\n    max 3\\n  outcome approved: when verdict.approved, route done(verdict)\\n  outcome refused: otherwise, route stuck(request)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 2,\n        \"title\": \"Values and types\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §2 — Values and types\",\n      \"form\": \"condition-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"condition\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"const\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Declares a compile-time constant, which may never depend on a runtime binding.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! A compile-time constant, usable anywhere a value is.\\nworkflow announce_poll\\n  input feed: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nconst prefix = \\\"polling: \\\"\\n\\nstep announce\\n  route done(prefix + feed)\",\n        \"//! A multi-line constant: instructions for an agent seam.\\nworkflow brief_agent\\n  input goal: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nconst instructions = \\\"\\\"\\\"\\nWork from repository evidence only.\\nReturn one paragraph.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\ntype Reply { text: String, final_message: String, stop_reason: String, session_id: String }\\n\\nworker assistants\\n  action ask(prompt: String) -> Reply\\n    agent, timeout 10m\\n\\nstep ask\\n  ask(prompt: instructions + goal) -> reply\\n  route done(reply.text)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 2,\n        \"title\": \"Values and types\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §2 — Values and types\",\n      \"form\": \"declaration\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"top-level\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"json\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Opens a literal JSON body, as in `json { \\\"type\\\": \\\"string\\\" }`, and names the JSON capture selector in a worker document's `run json \\\"…\\\"` body — two distinct places.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `json { … }` opens a literal JSON body in constant position.\\nworkflow request_review\\n  input diff: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nconst output_schema = json { \\\"type\\\": \\\"string\\\" }\\n\\nworker reviewers\\n  action review(prompt: String, contract: String) -> String\\n\\nstep review\\n  review(prompt: diff, contract: output_schema) -> verdict\\n  route done(verdict)\",\n        \"//! `run json` names the capture selector: stdout parses into the type.\\nworker probes\\n  action host_report(host: String) -> Report\\n    run json \\\"host-tool --json --host $host\\\"\\n\\ntype Report { cpu_count: Int, host: String }\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 2,\n        \"title\": \"Values and types\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §2 — Values and types\",\n      \"form\": \"value-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"const-value\",\n        \"run-capture\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"of\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Names the subject of `schema of Type`, the constant-position schema derivation.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `schema of Type` derives a schema constant from a declared type.\\nworkflow plan_items\\n  input goal: String\\n  outcome done: type Plan, route success\\n\\nconst plan_schema = schema of Plan\\n\\ntype Plan { items: [String] }\\n\\nworker agents\\n  action plan(prompt: String, output_schema: String) -> Plan\\n\\nstep plan\\n  plan(prompt: goal, output_schema: plan_schema) -> result\\n  route done(result)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 2,\n        \"title\": \"Values and types\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §2 — Values and types\",\n      \"form\": \"value-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"schema-source\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"subflow\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Declares an inline reusable graph with typed parameters and exactly one outcome, compiled into this workflow.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! An inline reusable graph with typed parameters and one outcome.\\nworkflow review_repo\\n  input repo: String\\n  outcome done: type Report, route success\\n\\ntype Report { summary: String }\\n\\nworker reviewers\\n  action inspect(target: String) -> Report\\n\\nsubflow review_once(target: String)\\n  outcome out: type Report\\n\\n  step look\\n    inspect(target: target) -> report\\n    route out(report)\\n\\nstep review\\n  review_once(target: repo) -> report\\n\\nstep publish\\n  report |> route done\",\n        \"//! One subflow, run once per item inside a distribute region.\\nworkflow review_wave\\n  input repos: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [Verdict], route success\\n\\ntype Verdict { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker reviewers\\n  action inspect(target: String) -> Verdict\\n\\nsubflow review_one(target: String)\\n  outcome out: type Verdict\\n\\n  step look\\n    inspect(target: target) -> verdict\\n    route out(verdict)\\n\\nstep open\\n  distribute repo in repos\\n\\nstep review\\n  review_one(target: repo) -> verdict\\n\\nstep close\\n  collect verdict -> verdicts\\n  route done(verdicts)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 5,\n        \"title\": \"Children and subflows\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §5 — Children and subflows\",\n      \"form\": \"declaration\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"top-level\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"distribute\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Opens a multi-step region that runs one item track per element in parallel.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Every host gets its own step track; the tracks run in parallel.\\nworkflow inspect_fleet\\n  input hosts: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [Report], route success\\n\\ntype Report { host: String }\\n\\nworker ops\\n  action inspect(host: String) -> Report\\n\\nstep open\\n  distribute host in hosts\\n\\nstep work\\n  inspect(host: host) -> report\\n\\nstep close\\n  collect report -> reports\\n  route done(reports)\",\n        \"//! Each item track is a little graph: inspect, then verify.\\nworkflow remediate_fleet\\n  input hosts: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [Verdict], route success\\n\\ntype Fault   { detail: String }\\ntype Verdict { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker ops\\n  action inspect(host: String) -> Fault\\n  action verify(host: String, fault: Fault) -> Verdict\\n\\nstep open\\n  distribute host in hosts\\n\\nstep inspect_host\\n  inspect(host: host) -> fault\\n\\nstep verify_host\\n  verify(host: host, fault: fault) -> verdict\\n\\nstep close\\n  collect verdict -> verdicts\\n  route done(verdicts)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 8,\n        \"title\": \"Parallel and sequential work\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §8 — Parallel and sequential work\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"sequence\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Opens a multi-step region that runs one item track at a time in collection order.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! One host at a time, in collection order, each through its own track.\\nworkflow roll_out\\n  input hosts: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [Attempt], route success\\n\\ntype Attempt { host: String, ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker ops\\n  action deploy(host: String) -> Attempt\\n\\nstep ordered\\n  sequence host in hosts\\n\\nstep push\\n  deploy(host: host) -> attempt\\n\\nstep settle\\n  collect attempt -> attempts\\n  route done(attempts)\",\n        \"//! Ordered rollout with an in-track retry before each host settles.\\nworkflow canary_rollout\\n  input hosts: [String]\\n  outcome done: type Int, route success\\n\\ntype Attempt { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker ops\\n  action deploy(host: String) -> Attempt\\n  action verify(attempt: Attempt) -> Attempt\\n\\nstep ordered\\n  sequence host in hosts\\n\\nstep push after ordered\\n  deploy(host: host) -> attempt\\n\\nstep confirm\\n  verify(attempt: attempt) -> checked\\n  outcome retry: when not checked.ok and visits < 3, route push\\n  outcome move_on: otherwise, route settle\\n  max 3 visits\\n\\nstep settle\\n  collect checked -> attempts\\n  attempts |> count -> rolled\\n  route done(rolled)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 8,\n        \"title\": \"Parallel and sequential work\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §8 — Parallel and sequential work\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"collect\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Closes the nearest region as the first statement of its close step, binding results in input order.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `collect` opens the close step, folding the region's results back in.\\nworkflow check_services\\n  input services: [String]\\n  outcome done: type Int, route success\\n\\ntype Health { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker probes\\n  action probe(service: String) -> Health\\n\\nstep open\\n  distribute service in services\\n\\nstep probe_one\\n  probe(service: service) -> health\\n\\nstep close\\n  collect health -> results\\n  results |> count -> total\\n  route done(total)\",\n        \"//! Tolerant collect: one optional slot per item, failures kept in history.\\nworkflow best_effort_sweep\\n  input repos: [String]\\n  outcome done: type Int, route success\\n\\ntype Report { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker ci\\n  action check(repo: String) -> Report\\n\\nstep open\\n  distribute repo in repos\\n\\nstep check_repo\\n  check(repo: repo) -> report\\n\\nstep close\\n  collect report? -> reports\\n  reports |> count -> total\\n  route done(total)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 8,\n        \"title\": \"Parallel and sequential work\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §8 — Parallel and sequential work\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"visits\",\n      \"kind\": \"keyword\",\n      \"summary\": \"Completes a step's `max N visits` bound, and reads as the visit count inside that step's guards.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `max 3 visits` bounds the flow loop; `visits` reads the count in guards.\\nworkflow recheck_repo\\n  input repo: String\\n  outcome done: type Verdict, route success\\n\\ntype Verdict { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker ci\\n  action check(repo: String) -> Verdict\\n\\nstep attempt\\n  check(repo: repo) -> verdict\\n\\nstep judge\\n  outcome again: when not verdict.ok and visits < 3, route attempt\\n  outcome settled: otherwise, route done(verdict)\\n  max 3 visits\",\n        \"//! The visit bound can be an expression over inputs and consts.\\nworkflow bounded_polish\\n  input text: String\\n  input max_rounds: Int\\n  outcome done: type Verdict, route success\\n\\ntype Verdict { ok: Bool }\\n\\nworker desks\\n  action polish(text: String) -> Verdict\\n\\nstep attempt\\n  polish(text: text) -> verdict\\n\\nstep judge\\n  outcome again: when not verdict.ok and visits < max_rounds, route attempt\\n  outcome settled: otherwise, route done(verdict)\\n  max max_rounds visits\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 9,\n        \"title\": \"Bounded iteration\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §9 — Bounded iteration\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"send\",\n      \"kind\": \"word\",\n      \"summary\": \"Declares an outbound signal in the header, and in a step body sends it to another running workflow.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! Signal another running workflow, addressed by its durable id.\\nworkflow report_progress\\n  input coordinator_id: String\\n  input item: String\\n  send progress: Progress\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Progress { item: String, state: String }\\n\\nstep announce\\n  send progress(item: item, state: \\\"started\\\") to coordinator_id\\n  route done(item)\",\n        \"//! Three payload forms: a record, a bare value, and `Nil` with nothing.\\nworkflow announce_gates\\n  input coordinator_id: String\\n  input item: String\\n  send heartbeat: Nil\\n  send progress: Progress\\n  send verdict: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Progress { item: String, state: String }\\n\\nstep announce\\n  send heartbeat to coordinator_id\\n  send progress(item: item, state: \\\"gated\\\") to coordinator_id\\n  send verdict(\\\"ok: \\\" + item) to coordinator_id\\n  route done(item)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 12,\n        \"title\": \"Sending signals to other workflows\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §12 — Sending signals to other workflows\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"workflow-header\",\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"to\",\n      \"kind\": \"word\",\n      \"summary\": \"Addresses the target of a `send`: a workflow id, which is a `String`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `to` addresses the target: a workflow id, which is a String.\\nworkflow notify_parent\\n  input parent_id: String\\n  input item: String\\n  send finished: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep notify\\n  send finished(\\\"done: \\\" + item) to parent_id\\n  route done(item)\",\n        \"//! Any bound String can follow `to` — even this run's own id.\\nworkflow self_note\\n  input note: String\\n  send reminder: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep remind\\n  send reminder(note) to workflow.id\\n  route done(note)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 12,\n        \"title\": \"Sending signals to other workflows\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §12 — Sending signals to other workflows\",\n      \"form\": \"target-word\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"signal-reference\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"query\",\n      \"kind\": \"word\",\n      \"summary\": \"Declares what an operator may ask a running instance, and the type of the answer they receive.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `query` declares what an operator may ask a running instance.\\nworkflow track_import\\n  input dataset: String\\n  query phase: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep ingest\\n  answer phase(\\\"ingesting \\\" + dataset)\\n  route done(dataset)\",\n        \"//! Query types are wire types: Nil, lists, and optionals all serve.\\nworkflow watch_harvest\\n  input crop: String\\n  query heartbeat: Nil\\n  query notes: [String]\\n  query last_note: String?\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep harvest\\n  answer heartbeat\\n  answer notes([crop])\\n  answer last_note(crop)\\n  route done(crop)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 13,\n        \"title\": \"The read-only query surface\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §13 — The read-only query surface\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"workflow-header\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"answer\",\n      \"kind\": \"word\",\n      \"summary\": \"Registers the value a declared query returns, computed from the bindings in scope where it stands.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `answer` registers the value a declared query returns.\\nworkflow watch_backup\\n  input volume: String\\n  query progress: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\nstep copy\\n  answer progress(\\\"copying \\\" + volume)\\n  route done(volume)\",\n        \"//! Re-answer inside the loop: live progress for whoever asks.\\nworkflow long_review\\n  input item: String\\n  input max_passes: Int\\n  query status: String\\n  outcome done:  type Verdict, route success\\n  outcome stuck: type String,  route failure\\n\\ntype Verdict { approved: Bool }\\n\\nworker desks\\n  action evaluate(item: String) -> Verdict\\n\\nstep review\\n  loop verdict = Verdict(approved: false)\\n    answer status(\\\"reviewing \\\" + item)\\n    evaluate(item: item) -> verdict\\n    until verdict.approved\\n    max max_passes\\n  answer status(\\\"settled\\\")\\n  outcome approved: when verdict.approved, route done(verdict)\\n  outcome exhausted: otherwise, route stuck(item)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 13,\n        \"title\": \"The read-only query surface\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §13 — The read-only query surface\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"run\",\n      \"kind\": \"word\",\n      \"summary\": \"Gives an action a declared command body, which the server runs itself with no sandbox of any kind.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! The document is the implementation: the server runs the command itself.\\nworkflow greet_by_hand\\n  input name: String\\n  outcome done: type Stdout, route success\\n\\ntype Stdout { stdout: String }\\n\\nworker shell\\n  action greet(name: String) -> Stdout\\n    run \\\"echo hello $name\\\"\\n\\nstep greet\\n  greet(name: name) -> out\\n  route done(out)\",\n        \"//! `run text` returns the command's trimmed stdout as one String.\\nworker shell_tools\\n  action checksum(path: String) -> String\\n    run text \\\"shasum $path\\\"\",\n        \"//! `run json` parses the command's stdout into the declared type.\\nworker probes\\n  action disk_report(mount: String) -> Report\\n    run json \\\"disk-tool --json $mount\\\"\\n\\ntype Report { free_bytes: Int, mount: String }\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 4,\n        \"title\": \"Workers, actions, and calls\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §4 — Workers, actions, and calls\",\n      \"form\": \"header-clause\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"worker-block\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"word\": \"in\",\n      \"kind\": \"word\",\n      \"summary\": \"Names the per-item binding of a fork or a region opener, as in `fork item in items`.\",\n      \"examples\": [\n        \"//! `in` names the per-item binding: each branch sees one `name`.\\nworkflow greet_everyone\\n  input names: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [Greeting], route success\\n\\ntype Greeting { text: String }\\n\\nworker greeter\\n  action greet(name: String) -> Greeting\\n\\nstep greet_each\\n  fork name in names\\n    greet(name: name)\\n  join -> greetings\\n  route done(greetings)\",\n        \"//! The same `in` names the per-item binding of a region opener.\\nworkflow document_hosts\\n  input hosts: [String]\\n  outcome done: type [String], route success\\n\\nworker ops\\n  action describe(host: String) -> String\\n\\nstep open\\n  distribute host in hosts\\n\\nstep describe_host\\n  describe(host: host) -> description\\n\\nstep close\\n  collect description -> descriptions\\n  route done(descriptions)\"\n      ],\n      \"section\": {\n        \"number\": 8,\n        \"title\": \"Parallel and sequential work\"\n      },\n      \"citation\": \"AWL reference §8 — Parallel and sequential work\",\n      \"form\": \"statement\",\n      \"positions\": [\n        \"step-body\"\n      ]\n    }\n  ],\n  \"reference_text\": \"# AWL-REFERENCE.md — checked workflow authoring surface\\n\\nThis is the assistant's AWL reference: the workflow-language surface accepted\\nby the repository's `aion-awl` parser and checker. It is distilled from\\n`crates/aion-awl/src/`, checker-valid rev-2 fixtures, and deployed `.awl`\\nexamples. If an installed `aion awl check` disagrees, its diagnostic and that\\nbinary's source win on everything the check sees — but `check` runs parse and\\ntypecheck only, and a further band of refusals arrives only when the document\\nis compiled (§15).\\n\\nThat precedence rule is live, not theoretical. This document describes the\\nchecker on the repository's main branch. The most recent release cut (0.12.0)\\npredates the worker-document family (§16) and the embedded language guide\\n(`aion awl guide`), so an installed binary at 0.12.0 or earlier refuses those\\nsurfaces while accepting the rest. Upgrade or build from the repository before\\nconcluding a construct does not exist.\\n\\n## 1. Document structure\\n\\nOne `.awl` file is one document of one FAMILY, and its opening declaration says\\nwhich: `workflow <name>` declares a flow (this section and every section up to\\n§15), `worker <queue>` declares the implementation of one task queue (§16).\\nUse two-space indentation. The canonical order of a workflow document is:\\n\\n```awl\\n//! Required workflow narration.\\nworkflow workflow_name\\n  timeout 2h\\n  input request: Request\\n  signal approval: Approval\\n  send progress: Progress\\n  query status: Status\\n  outcome done:   type Result, route success\\n  outcome failed: type Failure, route failure\\n\\nconst max_rounds = 3\\n\\ntype Request { name: String }\\ntype Result  { message: String }\\ntype Failure { reason: String }\\ntype Approval { reviewer: String }\\ntype Progress { stage: String }\\ntype Status   { phase: String }\\n\\nworker queue_name\\n  action perform(request: Request) -> Result\\n    node worker_node, timeout 10m, retry 3 every 30s\\n\\nchild independent_review(request: Request) -> Result\\n\\nsubflow inline_review(request: Request)\\n  outcome out: type Result\\n  step run\\n    perform(request: request) -> result\\n    route out(result)\\n\\nstep first\\n  perform(request: request) -> result\\n  result |> route done\\n```\\n\\nThe sample illustrates declaration placement; adapt the flow rather than\\ncopying unused declarations. A workflow needs at least one terminal outcome and\\nmust not fall off the final step.\\n\\n`//!` lines immediately before `workflow` narrate the workflow. `///` lines\\ndocument the declaration or field that follows. `//` is an ordinary comment.\\nIdentifiers are `snake_case`; nominal type names and enum variants are\\n`TitleCase`.\\n\\n## 2. Values and types\\n\\nBuilt-ins are `Bool`, `Int`, `Float`, `String`, `Nil`, `Dir`, lists `[T]`, and\\noptional fields `T?`. Optional means absent; JSON `null` is invalid. `[T?]`\\ncannot be written as a declared type\\n(`header-types/invalid/list_element_optional.awl`), while `[T]?` means the\\nwhole field can be absent. The `[T?]` shape itself does exist in one place:\\na tolerant `collect binding? -> results` binds exactly it (§8). A `Bool` is\\nwritten `true` or `false`; both are reserved words.\\n\\nRecord and enum declarations:\\n\\n```awl\\ntype Finding { title: String, blocking: Bool, detail: String? }\\ntype Decision = Approve | Revise | Reject\\n```\\n\\nRecord construction and access:\\n\\n```awl\\nFinding(title: \\\"missing gate\\\", blocking: true)\\nreport.findings\\n```\\n\\nSchema doors:\\n\\n```awl\\ntype Existing = schema(\\\"schemas/existing.schema.json\\\")\\ntype Inline = schema {\\n  \\\"type\\\": \\\"object\\\",\\n  \\\"required\\\": [\\\"value\\\"],\\n  \\\"properties\\\": { \\\"value\\\": { \\\"type\\\": \\\"string\\\" } }\\n}\\n```\\n\\nImports resolve relative to the `.awl` document. Missing files, invalid JSON,\\nand null-admitting types are check errors, and unsupported schema structure is\\nrefused by name: `oneOf`, `anyOf`, `patternProperties`, and an external `$ref`\\neach carry their own diagnostic (`schema-doors/invalid/import_oneof.awl`,\\n`import_anyof.awl`, `import_patternprops.awl`, `import_external_ref.awl`,\\n`import_null_type.awl`). A `$ref` into the schema's own `$defs` is supported\\n(`schema-doors/valid/import_nested_defs.awl`).\\n\\nConstants are compile-time values:\\n\\n```awl\\nconst attempts = 3\\nconst instructions = \\\"\\\"\\\"\\nUse the repository evidence. Return only the requested result.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nconst report_schema = schema of Report\\nconst literal_schema = json { \\\"type\\\": \\\"string\\\" }\\n```\\n\\nConstants may use literals, lists, `json` blocks, `schema of Type`, and constant\\nconcatenation. They cannot depend on runtime bindings. `schema of Type` is a\\nconstant-position form only: in a step-body expression it checks clean but does\\nnot lower (§15) — bind it to a `const` and use the constant.\\n\\nDurations are positive integer literals with `s`, `m`, `h`, or `d`, such as\\n`30s`, `5m`, `2h`, and `7d`. `workflow.id` is the durable execution identifier.\\n\\n## 3. Workflow header\\n\\n- `timeout DURATION` sets the workflow-run maximum. It is optional.\\n- `input name: Type` is repeatable and defines the start contract.\\n- `signal name: Type` declares a durable external signal this workflow\\n  **receives**.\\n- `send name: Type` declares a signal this workflow **sends** to another\\n  workflow (§12). Receiving and sending the same name are separate\\n  declarations; a document may carry both.\\n- `query name: Type` declares a read-only surface an operator can ask a\\n  RUNNING instance, and the type of the answer they get back (§13).\\n- `outcome name: type Type, route success|failure` declares a terminal payload\\n  and maps it to the engine's completed or failed status.\\n\\nTerminal routes must supply the declared outcome type, either by construction\\nor with an already-bound value of that type. The declared type is not\\nrestricted to records: builtins and lists are outcome types too, routed as\\nvalue payloads — `route done(note)` against `outcome done: type String`\\n(`flow-shape/valid/value_route_payload.awl`), or `type [String]`\\n(`flow-shape/valid/region_pure_decision.awl`). Subflow outcome declarations\\ntake the same type forms.\\n\\n## 4. Workers, actions, and calls\\n\\nA worker block imports an external worker contract:\\n\\n```awl\\nworker dev\\n  action build(repo: String, clean: Bool) -> Build\\n    node builder, timeout 30m, retry 2 backoff 10s..2m\\n```\\n\\nThe worker name (`dev`) is the task queue. Every worker has at least one action.\\nAction parameters and return types are checked. Calls use named arguments only:\\n\\n```awl\\nbuild(repo: request.repo, clean: true) -> built\\n```\\n\\nNo binding is required when only the action effect matters.\\n\\n### The config line\\n\\nAn action declaration may carry at most ONE indented config line, whose\\ncomma-separated keys are exactly:\\n\\n| Key | Form | Meaning |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| `advisory` | bare marker | The action's failure never faults its calling step, and it is not a completion dependency of its layer. Because it can promise no value, an advisory call cannot bind a result or serve as a pipe stage (§6). |\\n| `agent` | bare marker | The action is an agent seam (below). |\\n| `node` | `node NAME` | Pins dispatch to a worker node label. |\\n| `timeout` | `timeout DURATION` | Per-activity timeout. |\\n| `retry` | `retry N every DURATION` or `retry N backoff MIN..MAX` | Retry policy. |\\n\\n`advisory` and `agent` classify the DECLARATION: an action either is one or is\\nnot, so neither can be declared or overridden at a call site. A call site may\\noverride `node` and `timeout` only, as an indented config line after the call:\\n\\n```awl\\nbuild(repo: request.repo, clean: true) -> built\\n  node urgent, timeout 5m\\n```\\n\\n### Agent seams\\n\\n`agent` says exactly one thing: the action's single `String` parameter carries\\na PROMPT and its result is the canonical agent-outcome record — a record you\\ndeclare yourself, under any name, with exactly four fields: `text: String`\\n(the whole accumulated output), `final_message: String` (the last completed\\nagent answer alone, exactly as said), `stop_reason: String` (the canonical\\nsnake_case stop reason: `end_turn | tool_use | limit_reached | cancelled |\\nerror | other`), and `session_id: String` (the handle of the conversation that\\nproduced the outcome — a fact the harness fills, not a promise the session\\ncan be resumed; it is meaningful only to the harness that minted it). That is\\nthe whole of what an adapter needs to hand the call to an\\nagent harness instead of running it as an ordinary activity, and it is the\\nshape every harness emits: each maps its native terminal outcome onto the\\nrecord at its own boundary, so the flow reads `reply.text` — and can route on\\n`reply.stop_reason` — without ever parsing harness-specific JSON.\\n\\n```awl\\n//! Ask an agent one question and route its reply text.\\nworkflow ask_the_agent\\n  input question: String\\n  outcome done: type String, route success\\n\\ntype Reply { text: String, final_message: String, stop_reason: String, session_id: String }\\n\\nworker assistants\\n  action ask(prompt: String) -> Reply\\n    agent, timeout 10m\\n\\nstep ask\\n  ask(prompt: question) -> reply\\n  route done(reply.text)\\n```\\n\\nThe checker enforces the shape: exactly one parameter, that parameter `String`\\n(not `String?`), the return type a declared record of exactly `text: String`,\\n`final_message: String`, `stop_reason: String`, and `session_id: String` (empty\\n`text` and `final_message` values on a completed tool-only run are honest — the\\nagent said nothing; one answer makes them equal; an empty `session_id` is\\nrefused at the harness boundary — a harness that ran a conversation can name\\nit), and never\\ntogether with a `run` body — the server runs a declared body itself and never\\nhands it to a harness. The marker classifies the declaration and must not\\nchange the flow: `agent_seam.awl` and `agent_seam_unmarked_control.awl` must\\nlower to identical MIR.\\n\\n`aion worker agent <FILE.awl>` serves these seams without you writing a crate,\\nlaunching the agent from the `harness` section the same `worker` block carries\\n(§16).\\n\\n### Declared command bodies\\n\\nAn action may carry a `run` body, which means **the document is the\\nimplementation**: the server executes the command itself and no worker serves\\nthat action.\\n\\n```awl\\ntype RunOutcome { exit_code: Int, stdout: String, stderr: String }\\ntype Stdout     { stdout: String }\\ntype Greeting   { text: String }\\n\\nworker greeter\\n  /// The document IS the implementation.\\n  action greet(name: String) -> RunOutcome\\n    run \\\"echo hello $name\\\"\\n  /// No body: an out-of-band worker must serve this one.\\n  action polish(text: String) -> Greeting\\n  /// A body and a config line coexist; the config line prints first.\\n  action shout(text: String, mark: String) -> Stdout\\n    node shell, timeout 30s\\n    run \\\"printf %s%s ${text} $mark\\\"\\n```\\n\\nRules the checker enforces:\\n\\n- **One body per action.** There is no multi-command form. To run several\\n  commands, name a shell explicitly — `run \\\"sh -c 'a | b'\\\"` — which is visible\\n  in review, and that is the point.\\n- **The argument is one quoted string.** `run \\\"<command>\\\"`.
